# हिन्दी — Hindi / موطأ مالك — Muwatta of Imam Malik

## Narration 226

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A227)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 5</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، عَنْ سَالِمِ بْنِ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ، أَنَّهُ قَالَ دَخَلَ رَجُلٌ مِنْ أَصْحَابِ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم الْمَسْجِدَ يَوْمَ الْجُمُعَةِ وَعُمَرُ بْنُ الْخَطَّابِ يَخْطُبُ فَقَالَ عُمَرُ أَيَّةُ سَاعَةٍ هَذِهِ فَقَالَ يَا أَمِيرَ الْمُؤْمِنِينَ انْقَلَبْتُ مِنَ السُّوقِ فَسَمِعْتُ النِّدَاءَ فَمَا زِدْتُ عَلَى أَنْ تَوَضَّأْتُ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ عُمَرُ وَالْوُضُوءَ أَيْضًا وَقَدْ عَلِمْتَ أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم كَانَ يَأْمُرُ بِالْغُسْلِ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 227]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/227" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Ibn Shihab that Salim ibn Abdullah said, &quot;One of the companions of the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, came into the mosque on the day of jumua and Umar ibn al Khattab was already giving the khutba. Umar said, &#x27;What (kind of) time is this (to arrive)?&#x27; He said, Amir al- muminin, I returned from the market and heard the call to prayer, so I did no more than do wudu.&#x27; Umar said, &#x27;You only did wudu as well? You know that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, used to tell people to do ghusl</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 227]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/227" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">सलीम बिन अब्दुल्लाह से रिवायत है के उन्होंने कहा एक शख्स आये असहाब में से रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) की मस्जिद में जुम्मा के दिन और हज़रत उमर (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) खुतबा पढ़ रहे थे। तो बोले हज़रत उमर (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु), क्या वक्त है ये आने का? जवाब दिया उस शख्स ने के मैं लौटा बाज़ार से तो सुना मैंने अज़ान को, पस वज़ू किया और चला आया। तो कहा हज़रत उमर (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) ने, ये दूसरा कसूर है, तुमने सिर्फ वज़ू किया हालाँकि तुमको मालूम है के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) हुकुम करते थे ग़ुस्ल का।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 227]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/227" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">سالم بن عبداللہ سے روایت ہے کہ انہوں نے کہا ایک شخص آئے اصحاب میں سے رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کی مسجد میں جمعہ کے دن اور حضرت عمر خطبہ پڑھ رہے تھے تو بولے حضرت عمر کیا وقت ہے یہ آنے کا جواب دیا اس شخص نے کہ میں لوٹا بازار سے تو سنا میں نے اذان کو پس وضو کیا اور چلا آیا تو کہا حضرت عمر نے یہ دوسرا قصور ہے تم نے صرف وضو کیا حالانکہ تم کو معلوم ہے کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم حکم کرتے تھے غسل کا ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 227]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/227" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Salim bin Abdullah se riwayat hai ke unhone kaha ek shakhs aaye ashab mein se Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ki masjid mein Jummah ke din aur Hazrat Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) khutba padh rahe the. To bole Hazrat Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu), kya waqt hai ye aane ka? Jawab diya us shakhs ne ke main lauta bazaar se to suna maine azaan ko, pas wudu kiya aur chala aaya. To kaha Hazrat Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne, ye dusra qasoor hai, tumne sirf wudu kiya halankeh tumko maloom hai ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) hukum karte the ghusl ka.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 227]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/227" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 227

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A228)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 5</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ صَفْوَانَ بْنِ سُلَيْمٍ، عَنْ عَطَاءِ بْنِ يَسَارٍ، عَنْ أَبِي سَعِيدٍ الْخُدْرِيِّ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ غُسْلُ يَوْمِ الْجُمُعَةِ وَاجِبٌ عَلَى كُلِّ مُحْتَلِمٍ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 228]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/228" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 228</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Safwan ibn Sulaym from Ata ibn Yasar from Abu Said al-Khudri that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;Ghusl on the day of jumua is incumbent on every male who has reached puberty</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 228]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/228" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अबू सईद खुदरी (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: ग़ुस्ल जुम्मः का वाजिब है हर शख्स बालिग़ पर.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 228]</p>

## Narration 228

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A229)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 5</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ نَافِعٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ عُمَرَ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ إِذَا جَاءَ أَحَدُكُمُ الْجُمُعَةَ فَلْيَغْتَسِلْ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 229]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/229" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 229</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   229 ]</p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Nafi from Ibn Umar that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;When you come to jumua, do ghusl.&quot; Malik said, &quot;It is not enough for someone to do a ghusl on the day of jumua and intend by it the ghusl for jumua unless he does the ghusl and then sets off. That is because the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said in the hadith related by Ibn Umar, &#x27;When you come to jumua, do ghusl .&#x27; &quot; Malik said, &quot;If someone does ghusl on the day of jumua and intends by it the ghusl of the day of jumua and then sets out, whether early or late, and does something which breaks his wudu, he only has to do wudu and his ghusl remains valid for him</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 229]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/229" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="auto">अब्दुल्लाह बिन उमर (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है के फ़रमाया रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने: जो शख्स आये जुम्मा को तो ग़ुस्ल करके आये, या जो शख्स नमाज़-ए-जुम्मा का इरादा करे तो ग़ुस्ल कर ले।</p>

## Narration 229

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A230)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 5</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ أَبِي الزِّنَادِ، عَنِ الأَعْرَجِ، عَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ إِذَا قُلْتَ لِصَاحِبِكَ أَنْصِتْ وَالإِمَامُ يَخْطُبُ يَوْمَ الْجُمُعَةِ فَقَدْ لَغَوْتَ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 230]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/230" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Abu&#x27;z Zinad from al-Araj from Abu Hurayra that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;Even saying to your companion &#x27;Listen&#x27; while the imam is giving the khutba on the day of jumua, is to speak foolishly</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 230]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/230" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="auto">अबू हुरैरा (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है के फ़रमाया रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने: जिस वक्त इमाम खुतबा पढ़ता है अगर तू अपने पास वाले से कहे &#x27;चुप रह&#x27;, तो तूने भी एक लग्व हरकत की।</p>

## Narration 230

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A231)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 5</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: historical report. Attribution: عُمَرَ بْنِ الْخَطَّابِ (companion). The witnessed Friday-prayer practice in Umar’s time is selected. Ibn Shihab’s following general legal inference remains source context.</p>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، عَنْ ثَعْلَبَةَ بْنِ أَبِي مَالِكٍ الْقُرَظِيِّ، أَنَّهُ أَخْبَرَهُ أَنَّهُمْ، كَانُوا فِي زَمَانِ عُمَرَ بْنِ الْخَطَّابِ يُصَلُّونَ يَوْمَ الْجُمُعَةِ حَتَّى يَخْرُجَ عُمَرُ فَإِذَا خَرَجَ عُمَرُ وَجَلَسَ عَلَى الْمِنْبَرِ وَأَذَّنَ الْمُؤَذِّنُونَ - قَالَ ثَعْلَبَةُ - جَلَسْنَا نَتَحَدَّثُ فَإِذَا سَكَتَ الْمُؤَذِّنُونَ وَقَامَ عُمَرُ يَخْطُبُ أَنْصَتْنَا فَلَمْ يَتَكَلَّمْ مِنَّا أَحَدٌ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 231]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/231" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 231</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   231 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl"> قَالَ ابْنُ شِهَابٍ فَخُرُوجُ الإِمَامِ يَقْطَعُ الصَّلاَةَ وَكَلاَمُهُ يَقْطَعُ الْكَلاَمَ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/231" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: historical report. Attribution: Umar ibn al Khattab (companion). The witnessed Friday-prayer practice in Umar’s time is selected. Ibn Shihab’s following general legal inference remains source context.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Ibn Shihab that Thalaba ibn Abi Malik al Quradhi informed him that in the time of Umar ibn al Khattab they used to pray on the day of jumua until Umar came out, and when Umar came out and sat on the mimbar and the muadhdhins called the adhan, they would sit and talk, and then when the muadhdhins were silent and Umar stood to give the khutba, they would pay attention and no-one would speak .</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 231]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/231" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 231</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   231 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr"> Ibn Shihab said, &quot;The imam coming out stops prayer and his speaking stops conversation</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/231" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">सलबा बिन अबी मालिक अल-क़ुर्ज़ी से रिवायत है के लोग जुम्मा के दिन नमाज़ पढ़ा करते थे यहाँ तक के उमर बिन खत्ताब बाहर निकलते, फिर जब उमर निकलते और बैठ जाते तोह लोग बातें किया करते थे। जब मुअज़्ज़िन चुप हो जाता और उमर खड़े होकर खुतबा पढ़ते तोह फिर कोई बात न करता। इब्न शहाब ने कहा के जब इमाम खुतबे के लिए निकल आये तोह नमाज़ मौक़ूफ़ (बंद) कर देनी चाहिए और जब खुतबा शुरू हो जाये तोह बात चीत बंद कर देनी चाहिए।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/231" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: historical report. Attribution: عمر بن خطاب (companion). The witnessed Friday-prayer practice in Umar’s time is selected. Ibn Shihab’s following general legal inference remains source context.</p>

<p dir="rtl">ثعلبہ بن ابی مالک قرظی سے روایت ہے کہ لوگ نماز پڑھا کرتے تھے جمعہ کے دن یہاں تک کہ نکلیں عمر بن خطاب پھر جب نکلتے عمر اور بیٹھے ہوئے باتیں کیا کرتے جب مؤذن چپ ہو رہتے اور عمر کھڑے ہو کر خطبہ پڑھتے تو کوئی بات نہ کرتا</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 231]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/231" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Thalaba bin Abi Malik Al-Qurzi se riwayat hai ke log Jumma ke din namaz padha karte the yahan tak ke Umar bin Khattab bahar nikalte, phir jab Umar nikalte aur baith jate toh log batein kiya karte the. Jab Muazzin chup ho jata aur Umar khade hokar khutba padhte toh phir koi baat na karta. Ibn Shihab ne kaha ke jab Imam khutbe ke liye nikal aaye toh namaz moqoof (band) kar deni chahiye aur jab khutba shuru ho jaye toh baat cheet band kar deni chahiye.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/231" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

## Narration 231

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A232)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 5</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: عُثْمَانَ بْنَ عَفَّانَ (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ أَبِي النَّضْرِ، مَوْلَى عُمَرَ بْنِ عُبَيْدِ اللَّهِ عَنْ مَالِكِ بْنِ أَبِي عَامِرٍ، أَنَّ عُثْمَانَ بْنَ عَفَّانَ، كَانَ يَقُولُ فِي خُطْبَتِهِ قَلَّمَا يَدَعُ ذَلِكَ إِذَا خَطَبَ إِذَا قَامَ الإِمَامُ يَخْطُبُ يَوْمَ الْجُمُعَةِ فَاسْتَمِعُوا وَأَنْصِتُوا فَإِنَّ لِلْمُنْصِتِ الَّذِي لاَ يَسْمَعُ مِنَ الْحَظِّ مِثْلَ مَا لِلْمُنْصِتِ السَّامِعِ فَإِذَا قَامَتِ الصَّلاَةُ فَاعْدِلُوا الصُّفُوفَ وَحَاذُوا بِالْمَنَاكِبِ فَإِنَّ اعْتِدَالَ الصُّفُوفِ مِنْ تَمَامِ الصَّلاَةِ ‏.‏ ثُمَّ لاَ يُكَبِّرُ حَتَّى يَأْتِيَهُ رِجَالٌ قَدْ وَكَّلَهُمْ بِتَسْوِيَةِ الصُّفُوفِ فَيُخْبِرُونَهُ أَنْ قَدِ اسْتَوَتْ فَيُكَبِّرُ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 232]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/232" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Uthman ibn Affan (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Abu&#x27;n Nadr, the mawla of Umar ibn Ubaydullah, from Malik ibn Abi Amir that Uthman ibn Affan used to say in khutbas, and he would seldom omit it if he was giving the khutba, &quot;When the imam stands delivering the khutba on the day of jumua, listen and pay attention, for there is the same portion for someone who pays attention but cannot hear as for someone who pays attention and hears. And when the iqama of the prayer is called, straighten your rows and make your shoulders adjacent to each other, because the straightening of the rows is part of the completion of the prayer.&quot; Then he would not say the takbir until some men who had been entrusted with straightening the rows came and told him that they were straight. Then he would say the takbir</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 232]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/232" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<details class="reading-references">

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 232</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   232 ]</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">मालिक बिन अबी आमिर से रिवायत है के उस्मान बिन अफ़्फ़ान (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) जब खुतबे के लिए खड़े होते तोह अक्सर यह कहा करते (बहुत कम ही इस बात को छोड़ते): &#x27;ऐ लोगो! जब इमाम खुतबे के लिए खड़ा हो जाये तोह खुतबा सुनो और चुप रहो, क्यूंकि जो शख्स चुप रहेगा और खुतबा उसको न सुनाई देगा, उसको भी उतना ही अजर मिलेगा जितना उस शख्स को जो चुप रहे और खुतबा उसको सुनाई दे। और जब नमाज़ की तकबीर हो तोह सफों को बराबर करो और कंधों (shoulders) को मिलाओ, क्यूंकि सफों को बराबर करना नमाज़ का ततम्मा (हिस्सा) है।&#x27; फिर उस्मान (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) तब तक तकबीर-ए-तहरीमा नहीं कहते थे जब तक वो लोग आकर ख़बर न दे देते जिन्हें उन्होंने सफों को बराबर करने पर मुक़र्रर किया था, तब तकबीर-ए-तहरीमा कहते थे।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/232" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: عثمان بن عفان (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="rtl">مالک بن ابی عامر سے روایت ہے کہ عثمان بن عفان جب خطبہ کو کھڑے ہوئے تو اکثر کہا کرتے بہت کم چھوڑ دیتے یہ بات اے لوگو! جب امام کھڑا ہو خطبہ کے لئے تو سنو خطبہ کو اور چپ رہو کیونکہ جو شخص چپ رہے گا اور خطبہ اس کو نہ سنائی دے گا اس کو بھی اتنا ہی اجر ملے گا جتنا اس شخص کو ملے گا جو چپ رہے اور خطبہ اس کو سنائی دے اور جب تکبیر ہو نماز کی تو برابر کرو صفوں کو اور برابر کرو مونڈھوں کو کیونکہ صفیں برابر کرنا نماز کا تتمہ ہے پھر تکبیر تحریمہ نہ کہتے تھے عثمان یہاں تک کہ خبر دیتے آکر ان کو وہ لوگ جن کو مقرر کیا تھا صفیں برابر کرنے پر اس بات کی صفیں برابر ہو گئیں اس وقت تکبیر تحریمہ کہتے تھے ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 232]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/232" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   232 ]</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Malik bin Abi Amir se riwayat hai ke Usman bin Affan (Radi Allahu Anhu) jab khutbe ke liye khade hote toh aksar yeh kaha karte (bohot kam hi is baat ko chhodte): &#x27;Aye logo! Jab Imam khutbe ke liye khada ho jaye toh khutba suno aur chup raho, kyunke jo shakhs chup rahega aur khutba usko na sunayi dega, usko bhi utna hi ajar milega jitna us shakhs ko jo chup rahe aur khutba usko sunayi de. Aur jab namaz ki takbeer ho toh safo&#x27;n ko barabar karo aur kandho&#x27;n (shoulders) ko milao, kyunke safo&#x27;n ko barabar karna namaz ka tatamma (hissa) hai.&#x27; Phir Usman (Radi Allahu Anhu) tab tak takbeer-e-tahreema nahi kehte the jab tak woh log aakar khabar na de dete jinhe unhone safo&#x27;n ko barabar karne par muqarrar kiya tha, tab takbeer-e-tahreema kehte the.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/232" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

## Narration 232

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A233)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 5</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: عَبْدَ اللَّهِ بْنَ عُمَرَ (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ نَافِعٍ، أَنَّ عَبْدَ اللَّهِ بْنَ عُمَرَ، رَأَى رَجُلَيْنِ يَتَحَدَّثَانِ وَالإِمَامُ يَخْطُبُ يَوْمَ الْجُمُعَةِ فَحَصَبَهُمَا أَنِ اصْمُتَا ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 233]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/233" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Abdullah ibn Umar (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Nafi that Abdullah ibn Umar saw two men talking while the imam was giving the khutba on the day of jumua and he threw pebbles at them to tell them to be quiet</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 233]</p>

## Narration 233

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A234)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 5</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: successor report. Attribution: سَعِيدَ بْنَ الْمُسَيَّبِ (successor). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، أَنَّهُ بَلَغَهُ أَنَّ رَجُلاً، عَطَسَ يَوْمَ الْجُمُعَةِ وَالإِمَامُ يَخْطُبُ فَشَمَّتَهُ إِنْسَانٌ إِلَى جَنْبِهِ فَسَأَلَ عَنْ ذَلِكَ سَعِيدَ بْنَ الْمُسَيَّبِ فَنَهَاهُ عَنْ ذَلِكَ وَقَالَ لاَ تَعُدْ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 234]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/234" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: successor report. Attribution: Said ibn al Musayyab (successor). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that he had heard that a man sneezed on the day of jumua while the imam was giving the khutba, and a man by his side asked Allah to bless him. Said ibn al Musayyab was asked about it and he forbade the man to do what he had done and said, &quot;Don&#x27;t do it again</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 234]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/234" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">नाफ़े से रिवायत है के अब्दुल्लाह बिन उमर (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) ने दो मर्दों को खुतबे के वक़्त बातें करते देखा तो उन पर कंकर फेंके ताके वो चुप हो जाएं। इमाम मालिक तक यह बात पहुँची के एक शख्स ने जुमा के दिन छींक मारी और इमाम खुतबा पढ़ रहा था, तो एक आदमी ने उसको जवाब (यरहमुकल्लाह) दिया। फिर सईद बिन मुसय्यब से पूछा गया तो उन्होंने इससे मना किया और कहा के फिर ऐसा कभी ना करना।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/234" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="auto">This source entry preserves two separately attributed reports in their original order. They remain one provider entry, with their distinct narrator introductions and source references. Report type: companion report. Attribution: عبداللہ بن عمر (companion). Two Urdu source reports have distinct attributions and are retained as separate components under this provider occurrence. Report type: successor report. Attribution: سعید بن مسیب (successor). Two Urdu source reports have distinct attributions and are retained as separate components under this provider occurrence.</p>

<p class="counterpart-note">Source report 1</p>

<p dir="rtl">نافع سے روایت ہے کہ عبداللہ بن عمر نے دیکھا دو مردوں کو خطبہ کے وقت باتیں کر رہے ہیں تو کنکر پھینکے ان پر اس لئے کہ چپ رہیں ۔</p>

<p class="counterpart-note">Source report 2</p>

<p dir="rtl"> امام مالک کو پہنچا کہ ایک شخص چھینکا دن جمعہ کے اور امام خطبہ پڑھتا تھا تو جواب دیا اس کو ایک آدمی نے پھر پوچھا سعید بن مسیب سے تو منع کیا انہوں نے اس سے اور کہا کہ پھر ایسا نہ کرنا۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 234]</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Nafi se riwayat hai ke Abdullah bin Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne do mardo&#x27;n ko khutbe ke waqt baatein karte dekha toh un par kankar phenke taake woh chup ho jayein. Imam Malik tak yeh baat pahunchi ke ek shakhs ne Jumma ke din chheenk mari aur Imam khutba padh raha tha, toh ek aadmi ne usko jawab (Yarhamukallah) diya. Phir Saeed bin Musayyib se pucha gaya toh unhone isse mana kiya aur kaha ke phir aisa kabhi na karna.</p>

## Narration 234

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A235)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 5</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: successor report. Attribution: ابْنَ شِهَابٍ (successor). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، أَنَّهُ سَأَلَ ابْنَ شِهَابٍ عَنِ الْكَلاَمِ، يَوْمَ الْجُمُعَةِ إِذَا نَزَلَ الإِمَامُ عَنِ الْمِنْبَرِ، قَبْلَ أَنْ يُكَبِّرَ، ‏.‏ فَقَالَ ابْنُ شِهَابٍ لاَ بَأْسَ بِذَلِكَ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 235]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/235" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: successor report. Attribution: Ibn Shihab (successor). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that he asked Ibn Shihab about talking in the jumua after the imam had come down from the mimbar but before he had said the takbir. Ibn Shihab said, &quot;There is no harm in that</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 235]</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">इमाम मालिक से रिवायत है के उन्होंने इब्न शिहाब ज़ुहरी से पूछा, &#x27;जब इमाम खुतबा पढ़ कर मिंबर से उतरे तो तकबीर (इक़ामत) से पहले बात करना कैसा है?&#x27; तो इब्न शिहाब ने कहा, &#x27;इस में कोई क़बाहत नहीं है।&#x27;</p>

<p dir="auto">Report type: successor report. Attribution: ابن شہاب زہری (successor). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="rtl">امام مالک سے روایت ہے کہ انہوں نے پوچھا ابن شہاب زہری سے کہا جب امام منبر سے اترے خطبہ پڑھ کر تو قبل تکبیر کے بات کہنا کیسا ہے کہ ابن شہاب نے کچھ قباحت نہیں ہے ۔</p>

## Narration 235

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A236)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 5</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، أَنَّهُ كَانَ يَقُولُ مَنْ أَدْرَكَ مِنْ صَلاَةِ الْجُمُعَةِ رَكْعَةً فَلْيُصَلِّ إِلَيْهَا أُخْرَى ‏.‏ قَالَ ابْنُ شِهَابٍ وَهِيَ السُّنَّةُ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَعَلَى ذَلِكَ أَدْرَكْتُ أَهْلَ الْعِلْمِ بِبَلَدِنَا وَذَلِكَ أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ مَنْ أَدْرَكَ مِنَ الصَّلاَةِ رَكْعَةً فَقَدْ أَدْرَكَ الصَّلاَةَ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ فِي الَّذِي يُصِيبُهُ زِحَامٌ يَوْمَ الْجُمُعَةِ فَيَرْكَعُ وَلاَ يَقْدِرُ عَلَى أَنْ يَسْجُدَ حَتَّى يَقُومَ الإِمَامُ أَوْ يَفْرُغَ الإِمَامُ مِنْ صَلاَتِهِ إِنَّهُ إِنْ قَدَرَ عَلَى أَنْ يَسْجُدَ إِنْ كَانَ قَدْ رَكَعَ فَلْيَسْجُدْ إِذَا قَامَ النَّاسُ وَإِنْ لَمْ يَقْدِرْ عَلَى أَنْ يَسْجُدَ حَتَّى يَفْرُغَ الإِمَامُ مِنْ صَلاَتِهِ فَإِنَّهُ أَحَبُّ إِلَىَّ أَنْ يَبْتَدِئَ صَلاَتَهُ ظُهْرًا أَرْبَعًا ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 236]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/236" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 236</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   236 ]</p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that Ibn Shihab used to say, &quot;Some one who catches a raka of the jumua prayer should pray another one with it.&quot; Ibn Shihab said, &quot;That is the sunna.&quot; Malik said, &quot;I saw the people of knowledge in our city doing that. That is because the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &#x27;Whoever catches a raka of the prayer has caught the prayer.&#x27; &quot; Malik said, concerning some one who was in a crowd on the day of jumua and did the ruku but was not able to go into sajda until the imam had risen or finished his prayer, &quot;If he is able to do the sajda and has already done the ruku then he should do the sajda when the people stand up. If he is unable to do thesajda until after the imam has finished the prayer, then I prefer that he begins the prayeragain and does the four rakas of dhuhr</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 236]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/236" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">इब्न शिहाब से रिवायत है के वो कहते थे, &#x27;जो शख्स जुमा की नमाज़ की एक रकात पा ले, तो वो एक रकात अदा करे, यही सुन्नत है।&#x27;</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 236]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/236" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">ابن شہاب سے روایت ہے کہ وہ کہتے تھے جو شخص جمعہ کی نماز کی ایک رکعت پائے تو وہ ایک رکعت ادا کرے یہی سنت ہے ۔</p>

## Narration 236

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A237)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 5</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: عُمَرُ بْنُ الْخَطَّابِ (companion). This is a source-attributed report about Umar’s recitation, including the wording he is reported to recite. Surrounding Quran exposition and compiler legal discussion are separate context; no verse is labelled Prophetic speech.</p>

<p dir="rtl">فَقَالَ ابْنُ شِهَابٍ كَانَ عُمَرُ بْنُ الْخَطَّابِ يَقْرَؤُهَا إِذَا نُودِيَ لِلصَّلاَةِ مِنْ يَوْمِ الْجُمُعَةِ فَامْضُوا إِلَى ذِكْرِ اللَّهِ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 237]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/237" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 237</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   237 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، أَنَّهُ سَأَلَ ابْنَ شِهَابٍ عَنْ قَوْلِ اللَّهِ، عَزَّ وَجَلَّ ‏{‏يَا أَيُّهَا الَّذِينَ آمَنُوا إِذَا نُودِيَ لِلصَّلاَةِ مِنْ يَوْمِ الْجُمُعَةِ فَاسْعَوْا إِلَى ذِكْرِ اللَّهِ‏}‏ </p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/237" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<p dir="rtl"> قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَإِنَّمَا السَّعْىُ فِي كِتَابِ اللَّهِ الْعَمَلُ وَالْفِعْلُ يَقُولُ اللَّهُ تَبَارَكَ وَتَعَالَى ‏{‏وَإِذَا تَوَلَّى سَعَى فِي الأَرْضِ‏}‏ وَقَالَ تَعَالَى ‏{‏وَأَمَّا مَنْ جَاءَكَ يَسْعَى وَهُوَ يَخْشَى‏}‏ وَقَالَ ‏{‏ثُمَّ أَدْبَرَ يَسْعَى‏}‏ وَقَالَ ‏{‏إِنَّ سَعْيَكُمْ لَشَتَّى‏}‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ فَلَيْسَ السَّعْىُ الَّذِي ذَكَرَ اللَّهُ فِي كِتَابِهِ بِالسَّعْىِ عَلَى الأَقْدَامِ وَلاَ الاِشْتِدَادَ وَإِنَّمَا عَنَى الْعَمَلَ وَالْفِعْلَ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/237" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Umar ibn al-Khattab (companion). This is a source-attributed report about Umar’s recitation, including the wording he is reported to recite. Surrounding Quran exposition and compiler legal discussion are separate context; no verse is labelled Prophetic speech.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Ibn Shihab said, &#x27;&#x27;Umar ibn al-Khattab used to recite, &#x27;When the call is made for the prayer on the day of jumua, go to the remembrance of Allah.&#x27; &quot;</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 237]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/237" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 237</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   237 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Malik said, &quot;Someone who has a nosebleed on the day of jumua while the imam is giving the khutba and he leaves and does not come back until the imam has finished the prayer, should pray four rakas.&#x27;&#x27; Malik said that someone who prayed a raka with the imam on the day of jumua, then had a nosebleed and left and came back and the imam had prayed both rakas, should complete the prayer with another raka as long as he had not spoken. Malik said, &quot;If some one has a nosebleed, or something happens to him and he has no alternative but to leave, he does not have to ask permission of the imam if he wants to leave on the day of jumua.&quot; Yahya related to me from Malik that he had asked Ibn Shihab about the word of Allah, the Majestic, the Mighty, &quot;O you who accept, when the call is made for the prayer on the day of jumua, make haste to the remembrance of Allah.&quot;(Sura 62 ayat 9). </p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/237" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<p dir="ltr"> Malik said, &quot;Making haste in the Book of Allah is only deed and action. Allah the Blessed, the Exalted, says &#x27;and when he turns away, he acts in the land&#x27; (Sura 2 ayat 205), and He, the Exalted, said, &#x27;and as for the one who comes to you acting with fear&#x27; (Sura 80 ayat 8), and He said, &#x27;then he turned his back, and acted&#x27; (Sura 79 ayat 22),and He said, &#x27;Your deeds are diverse&#x27; &quot; (Sura 92 ayat 4). Malik said, &quot;Thus making haste which Allah mentions in His Book is not running on the feet or exertion. It only means deed and actions</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/237" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<p dir="auto">इमाम मलिक ने इब्न शिहाब से पूछा कि इस आयत की तफ़्सीर क्या है: &#x27;ईज़ानूरिया लिस्सालाती मं यौमिल जुमति फास&#x27;अउ इला ज़िकृल्लहि&#x27;? तोह इब्न शिहाब ने जवाब दिया की हज़रत उमर बिन खत्ताब (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) इस आयत को यूँ पढ़ते थे: &#x27;ईज़ानूरिया लिस्सालाती मं यौमिल जुमति फंडु (फास&#x27;अउ) इला ज़िकरीलाही&#x27;.</p>

## Narration 237

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A238)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 5</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ أَبِي الزِّنَادِ، عَنِ الأَعْرَجِ، عَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ذَكَرَ يَوْمَ الْجُمُعَةِ فَقَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ فِيهِ سَاعَةٌ لاَ يُوَافِقُهَا عَبْدٌ مُسْلِمٌ وَهُوَ قَائِمٌ يُصَلِّي يَسْأَلُ اللَّهَ شَيْئًا إِلاَّ أَعْطَاهُ إِيَّاهُ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ وَأَشَارَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم بِيَدِهِ يُقَلِّلُهَا ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 238]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/238" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 238</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   238 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Malik said, &quot;If the imam stops off on a journey in a town where jumua is obligatory and he gives a khutba and takes the jumua prayer for them, then the people of the town and any other people present do the jumua prayer with him.&quot; Malik said, &quot;If the imam gathers people for prayer while he is travelling in a town where the jumua prayer is not obligatory, then there is no jumua for him, nor for the people of the town, nor for anyone else who joins them for the prayer in congregation, and the people of the town and anyone else who is not travelling should complete the prayer.&quot; Malik added, &quot;A traveller does not have to do jumua &quot; Yahya related to me from Malik from Abu&#x27;z Zinad from al-Araj from Abu Hurayra that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, mentioned the day of jumua and said, &quot;There is a time in it when Allah gives to a muslim slave standing in prayer whatever he asks for,&quot; and the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, indicated with his hand how small it was</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 238]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/238" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 238</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   238 ]</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अबू हुरैरा (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने जुमा का ज़िक्र किया फिर कहा, &#x27;इस में एक साअत (घड़ी/वक़्त) ऐसी है के कोई मुसलमान बंदा उस वक़्त नमाज़ में खड़ा हो और अल्लाह से कुछ मांगे तो अल्लाह उसको ज़रूर देता है।&#x27; और रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने अपने हाथ से इशारा किया के वो साअत बहुत मुख़्तसर (छोटी) है।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 238]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/238" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">ابوہریرہ رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ روایت ہے کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے ذکر کیا جمعہ کا پھر کہا کہ اس میں ایک ساعت ایسی ہے کہ نہیں پاتا اس کا بندہ مسلمان اور وہ نماز میں کھڑا ہوتا ہے اور مانگتا ہے اللہ سے کچھ مگر دیتا ہے اللہ اس چیز کو اس کو اور اشارہ کیا رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے اپنے ہاتھ سے کہ وہ ساعت تھوڑی ہے ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 238]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/238" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Abu Huraira (Radi Allahu Anhu) se riwayat hai ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne Jumma ka zikr kiya phir kaha, &#x27;Is mein ek sa&#x27;at (ghadi/waqt) aisi hai ke koi musalman banda us waqt namaz mein khada ho aur Allah se kuch mange toh Allah usko zaroor deta hai.&#x27; Aur Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne apne hath se ishara kiya ke woh sa&#x27;at bohot mukhtasar (chhoti) hai.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 238]</p>

## Narration 238

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A239)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 5</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ يَزِيدَ بْنِ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ الْهَادِ، عَنْ مُحَمَّدِ بْنِ إِبْرَاهِيمَ بْنِ الْحَارِثِ التَّيْمِيِّ، عَنْ أَبِي سَلَمَةَ بْنِ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنِ عَوْفٍ، عَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ، أَنَّهُ قَالَ خَرَجْتُ إِلَى الطُّورِ فَلَقِيتُ كَعْبَ الأَحْبَارِ فَجَلَسْتُ مَعَهُ فَحَدَّثَنِي عَنِ التَّوْرَاةِ وَحَدَّثْتُهُ عَنْ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم فَكَانَ فِيمَا حَدَّثْتُهُ أَنْ قُلْتُ قَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏&quot;‏ خَيْرُ يَوْمٍ طَلَعَتْ عَلَيْهِ الشَّمْسُ يَوْمُ الْجُمُعَةِ فِيهِ خُلِقَ آدَمُ وَفِيهِ أُهْبِطَ مِنَ الْجَنَّةِ وَفِيهِ تِيبَ عَلَيْهِ وَفِيهِ مَاتَ وَفِيهِ تَقُومُ السَّاعَةُ وَمَا مِنْ دَابَّةٍ إِلاَّ وَهِيَ مُصِيخَةٌ يَوْمَ الْجُمُعَةِ مِنْ حِينِ تُصْبِحُ حَتَّى تَطْلُعَ الشَّمْسُ شَفَقًا مِنَ السَّاعَةِ إِلاَّ الْجِنَّ وَالإِنْسَ وَفِيهِ سَاعَةٌ لاَ يُصَادِفُهَا عَبْدٌ مُسْلِمٌ وَهُوَ يُصَلِّي يَسْأَلُ اللَّهَ شَيْئًا إِلاَّ أَعْطَاهُ إِيَّاهُ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ قَالَ كَعْبٌ ذَلِكَ فِي كُلِّ سَنَةٍ يَوْمٌ ‏.‏ فَقُلْتُ بَلْ فِي كُلِّ جُمُعَةٍ ‏.‏ فَقَرَأَ كَعْبٌ التَّوْرَاةَ فَقَالَ صَدَقَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏.‏ قَالَ أَبُو هُرَيْرَةَ فَلَقِيتُ بَصْرَةَ بْنَ أَبِي بَصْرَةَ الْغِفَارِيَّ فَقَالَ مِنْ أَيْنَ أَقْبَلْتَ فَقُلْتُ مِنَ الطُّورِ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ لَوْ أَدْرَكْتُكَ قَبْلَ أَنْ تَخْرُجَ إِلَيْهِ مَا خَرَجْتَ سَمِعْتُ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم يَقُولُ ‏&quot;‏ لاَ تُعْمَلُ الْمَطِيُّ إِلاَّ إِلَى ثَلاَثَةِ مَسَاجِدَ إِلَى الْمَسْجِدِ الْحَرَامِ وَإِلَى مَسْجِدِي هَذَا وَإِلَى مَسْجِدِ إِيلْيَاءَ أَوْ بَيْتِ الْمَقْدِسِ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ يَشُكُّ ‏.‏ قَالَ أَبُو هُرَيْرَةَ ثُمَّ لَقِيتُ عَبْدَ اللَّهِ بْنَ سَلاَمٍ فَحَدَّثْتُهُ بِمَجْلِسِي مَعَ كَعْبِ الأَحْبَارِ وَمَا حَدَّثْتُهُ بِهِ فِي يَوْمِ الْجُمُعَةِ فَقُلْتُ قَالَ كَعْبٌ ذَلِكَ فِي كُلِّ سَنَةٍ يَوْمٌ ‏.‏ قَالَ قَالَ عَبْدُ اللَّهِ بْنُ سَلاَمٍ كَذَبَ كَعْبٌ ‏.‏ فَقُلْتُ ثُمَّ قَرَأَ كَعْبٌ التَّوْرَاةَ فَقَالَ بَلْ هِيَ فِي كُلِّ جُمُعَةٍ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ عَبْدُ اللَّهِ بْنُ سَلاَمٍ صَدَقَ كَعْبٌ ‏.‏ ثُمَّ قَالَ عَبْدُ اللَّهِ بْنُ سَلاَمٍ قَدْ عَلِمْتُ أَيَّةَ سَاعَةٍ هِيَ ‏.‏ قَالَ أَبُو هُرَيْرَةَ فَقُلْتُ لَهُ أَخْبِرْنِي بِهَا وَلاَ تَضِنَّ عَلَىَّ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ عَبْدُ اللَّهِ بْنُ سَلاَمٍ هِيَ آخِرُ سَاعَةٍ فِي يَوْمِ الْجُمُعَةِ ‏.‏ قَالَ أَبُو هُرَيْرَةَ فَقُلْتُ وَكَيْفَ تَكُونُ آخِرُ سَاعَةٍ فِي يَوْمِ الْجُمُعَةِ وَقَدْ قَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏&quot;‏ لاَ يُصَادِفُهَا عَبْدٌ مُسْلِمٌ وَهُوَ يُصَلِّي ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ وَتِلْكَ السَّاعَةُ سَاعَةٌ لاَ يُصَلَّى فِيهَا فَقَالَ عَبْدُ اللَّهِ بْنُ سَلاَمٍ أَلَمْ يَقُلْ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏&quot;‏ مَنْ جَلَسَ مَجْلِسًا يَنْتَظِرُ الصَّلاَةَ فَهُوَ فِي صَلاَةٍ حَتَّى يُصَلِّيَ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ قَالَ أَبُو هُرَيْرَةَ فَقُلْتُ بَلَى ‏.‏ قَالَ فَهُوَ ذَلِكَ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 239]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/239" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Yazid ibn Abdullah ibn al-Had from Muhammad ibn Ibrahim ibn al-Harith at-Taymi from Abu Salama ibn Abd ar-Rahman ibn Awf that Abu Hurayra said, &quot;I went out to at-Tur (Mount Sinai) and met Kab al Ahbar and sat with him. He related to me things from the Tawrah and I related to him things from the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace. Among the things I related to him was that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &#x27;The best of days on which the sun rises is the day of jumua. In it Adam was created, and in it he fell from the Garden. In it he was forgiven, and in it he died. In it the Hour occurs, and every moving thing listens from morning till sunset in apprehension of the Hour except jinn and men. In it is a time when Allah gives toa muslim slave standing in prayer whatever he asks for.&#x27; Kab said, &#x27;That is one day in every year.&#x27; I said, &#x27;No, in every jumua.&#x27; Then Kab recited the Tawrah and said, &#x27;The Messenger of Allah has spoken the truth.&#x27; &quot; Abu Hurayra continued, &quot;I met Basra ibn Abi Basra al-Ghiffari and he said, &#x27;Where have you come from?&#x27; I said, &#x27;From at-Tur.&#x27; He said, &#x27;If I had seen you before you left, you would not have gone. I heard the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, say, &quot;Only make a special journey to three mosques:the mosque of the Haram (Makka), this mosque (Madina), and the mosque of Ilya or the Bait al-Maqdis (two names of Jerusalem).&quot; &#x27; &quot; (He was not sure which expression was used.) Abu Hurayra continued, &quot;Then I met Abdullah ibn Salam and I told him that I had sat with Kabal-Ahbar, and I mentioned what I had related to him about the day of jumua, and told him that Kab had said, &#x27;That is one day in every year.&#x27; Abdullah ibn Salam said, &#x27;Kab lied,&#x27; and I added, &#x27;Kab then recited the Tawrah and said, &quot;No, it is in every jumua.&#x27;&#x27; &#x27; Abdullah ibn Salam said, &#x27;Kab spoke the truth. &#x27;Then Abdullah ibn Salam said, &#x27;I know what time that is.&#x27; &quot; Abu Hurayra continued, &quot;I said to him, &#x27;Let me know it - don&#x27;t keep it from me.&#x27; Abdullah ibn Salam said, &#x27;It is the last period of time in the dayof jumua.&#x27; &quot; Abu Hurayra continued, &quot;I said, &#x27;How can it be the last period of time in the day of jumua, when the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;a muslim slave standing in prayer&quot;, and that is a time when there is no prayer?&#x27; Abdullah ibn Salam replied, &#x27;Didn&#x27;t the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, say, &quot;Whoever sits waiting for the prayer is in prayer until he prays?&quot; &quot;&#x27; Abu Hurayra added, &quot;I said, &#x27;Of course.&#x27; He said, &#x27;Then it is that</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 239]</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अबू हुरैरा (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) रिवायत करते हैं के मैं कोह-ए-तूर पर गया और काअब अल-अहबार से मिला, मैं उनके पास बैठा तो काअब ने मुझे तौरात की बातें बतायीं और मैंने उन्हें रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) की हदीसें सुनायीं। उन बातों में से एक यह थी के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया, &#x27;बेहतर दिन जिन में सूरज निकलता है जुम्मा का दिन है। इसी दिन आदम (अलैहिस सलाम) पैदा हुए, इसी दिन जन्नत से उतारे गए, इसी दिन उनका गुनाह माफ़ हुआ और इसी दिन क़यामत क़ायम होगी। कोई जानदार ऐसा नहीं जो जुम्मा के दिन आफ़ताब निकलने तक क़यामत के ख़ौफ़ से कान न लगाये, मगर जिन और आदमी ग़ाफ़िल रहते हैं। और जुम्मा में एक साअत ऐसी है के कोई मुसलमान बंदा नमाज़ में हो और अल्लाह से कुछ मांगे तो अल्लाह जल्ला जलालुहू उसको ज़रूर देता है।&#x27; काअब अल-अहबार ने कहा, &#x27;यह तो हर साल में एक दिन होता है।&#x27; मैंने कहा, &#x27;नहीं, बल्कि हर जुम्मा को।&#x27; तो काअब ने तौरात पढ़ी और कहा, &#x27;सच फ़रमाया रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने।&#x27; अबू हुरैरा (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) कहते हैं, फिर मैं बसरा बिन अबी बसरा ग़िफ़ारी से मिला, उन्होंने पूछा कहाँ से आ रहे हो? मैंने कहा कोह-ए-तूर से। उन्होंने कहा, &#x27;अगर तुम मुझे मिलते तो तुम वहाँ न जाते। मैंने रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) से सुना, आप फ़रमाते थे: &#x27;सिर्फ़ तीन मस्जिदों की तरफ़ ही सवारी की जाये: मस्जिदे हराम, मेरी यह मस्जिद और मस्जिदे इलिया या बैतुल मुक़द्दस&#x27; (रावी को शक है)। अबू हुरैरा (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) ने कहा, फिर मैं अब्दुल्लाह बिन सलाम से मिला और उन्हें वह गुफ़्तगू बतायी जो काअब से जुम्मा के बारे में हुई थी, और यह भी के काअब ने कहा था यह साल में एक बार होता है। अब्दुल्लाह बिन सलाम ने कहा, &#x27;काअब ने झूठ बोला।&#x27; फिर मैंने बताया के काअब ने तौरात पढ़ कर कहा के यह साअत हर जुम्मा को होती है, तो अब्दुल्लाह बिन सलाम ने कहा, &#x27;सच कहा काअब ने।&#x27; फिर अब्दुल्लाह बिन सलाम ने कहा, &#x27;मैं जानता हूँ वह साअत कौनसी है।&#x27; अबू हुरैरा (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) ने कहा, &#x27;मुझे बताओ और बख़ील न बनो।&#x27; अब्दुल्लाह बिन सलाम ने कहा, &#x27;वह जुम्मा की आख़िरी साअत है।&#x27; अबू हुरैरा (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) ने कहा, &#x27;वह आख़िरी साअत कैसे हो सकती है जबकि रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया है के मुसलमान बंदा नमाज़ में हो तभी मांगता है? यह साअत तो ऐसी है के इस में नमाज़ नहीं हो सकती?&#x27; अब्दुल्लाह बिन सलाम ने जवाब दिया, &#x27;क्या रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने नहीं फ़रमाया के जो शख़्स नमाज़ के इंतेज़ार में बैठा है वह नमाज़ में ही है जब तक नमाज़ न पढ़ ले?&#x27; अबू हुरैरा (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) ने कहा, &#x27;हाँ।&#x27; अब्दुल्लाह बिन सलाम ने कहा, &#x27;बस यही मतलब है।&#x27;</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 239]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/239" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">ابوہریرہ رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ روایت ہے کہ میں گیا کوہ طور پر تو ملا میں کعب بن الاحبار سے اور بیٹھا میں ان کے پاس پس بیان کیں کعب الاحبار نے مجھ سے باتیں تورات کی اور میں نے بیان کیں باتیں ان سے رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کی تو جو باتیں میں نے ان سے کہیں ان میں ایک یہ بھی تھی کہ فرمایا رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے بہتر سب دنوں میں جن میں سورج نکلا ہے جمعہ کا دن ہے اسی دن پیدا ہوئے آدم اور اسی دن اتارے گئے جنت سے اور اسی دن معاف ہوا گناہ ان کا اور اسی دن قیامت قائم ہوگی اور کوئی جاندار ایسا نہیں ہے جو کان نہ لگائے جمعہ کے دن آفتاب نکلنے تک قیامت کے خوف سے مگر جنات اور آدمی غافل رہتے ہیں اور جمعہ میں ایک ساعت ایسی ہے کہ نہیں پاتا اس کا مسلمان بندہ نماز میں اور وہ مانگے اللہ سے کچھ مگر دے اللہ جل جلالہ اس کو کعب الاحبار نے کہا یہ تو ہر سال میں ایک دن ہوتا ہے میں نے کہا نہیں بلکہ ہر جمعہ کو تو کعب نے تورات کو پڑھا پھر کہا سچ فرمایا رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے کہا ابوہریرہ نے پھر ملا میں بصرہ بن ابی بصرہ غفاری سے تو کہا انہوں نے کہاں سے آتے ہو میں نے کہا کہ کوہ طور سے کہا انہوں نے اگر قبل طور جانے کے تم مجھ سے ملتے تو تم نہ جاتے سنا میں نے رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم سے فرماتے تھے نہ تیار کئے جائیں اونٹ مگر تین مسجدوں کے لئے ایک مسجد الحرام دوسری میری مسجد یہ تیسری مسجد ایلیا یا بیت المقدس شک ہے راوی کو کہا ابوہریرہ نے پھر ملا میں عبداللہ بن سلام سے اور بیان کیا میں نے ان سے جو گفتگو کی تھی میں نے کعب الاحبار سے جمعہ کے باب میں اور میں نے یہ کہا کہ کعب الاحبار نے کہا یہ دن ہر سال میں ایک بار ہوتا ہے تو عبداللہ بن سلام نے کہا کہ جھوٹ بولا کعب نے پھر میں نے کہا کہ کعب نے تورات کو پڑھ کر یہ کہا کہ بے شک یہ ساعت ہر جمعہ کو ہوتی ہے تب عبداللہ بن سلام نے کہا کہ سچ کہا کعب نے پھر کہا عبداللہ بن سلام نے میں جانتا ہوں اس ساعت کو وہ کونسی ہے ابوہریرہ نے کہا کہ بتاؤ مجھ کو اور بخل نہ کرو عبداللہ بن سلام نے کہا کہ وہ آخر ساعت ہے جمعہ کی ابوہریرہ نے کہا کیونکہ آخر ساعت ہوگی حالانکہ فرمایا رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے کہا نہیں پاتا اس کو مسلمان بندہ نما میں مگر جو مانگتا ہے اللہ سے دیتا ہے اس کو یہ ساعت تو ایسی ہے کہ اس میں نماز نہیں ہو سکتی ہے تو جواب دیا عبداللہ بن سلام نے کیا نہیں فرمایا رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے کہ جو شخص بیٹھے نماز کے انتظار میں تو وہ نماز میں ہے یہاں تک کہ نماز پڑھے ابوہریرہ نے کہا ہاں عبداللہ بن سلام نے کہا پس یہی مطلب ہے ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 239]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/239" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Abu Huraira (Radi Allahu Anhu) riwayat karte hain ke main Koh-e-Toor par gaya aur Ka&#x27;ab Al-Ahbar se mila, main unke paas baitha toh Ka&#x27;ab ne mujhe Taurat ki baatein batayin aur maine unhe Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ki hadeesein sunayin. Un baaton mein se ek yeh thi ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya, &#x27;Behtar din jin mein suraj nikalta hai Jumma ka din hai. Isi din Adam (Alaihis Salam) paida hue, isi din jannat se utare gaye, isi din unka gunah maaf hua aur isi din qayamat qaim hogi. Koi jaandar aisa nahi jo Jumma ke din aaftab nikalne tak qayamat ke khauf se kaan na lagaye, magar jin aur aadmi ghafil rehte hain. Aur Jumma mein ek sa&#x27;at aisi hai ke koi musalman banda namaz mein ho aur Allah se kuch mange toh Allah Jalla Jalaluhu usko zaroor deta hai.&#x27; Ka&#x27;ab Al-Ahbar ne kaha, &#x27;Yeh toh har saal mein ek din hota hai.&#x27; Maine kaha, &#x27;Nahi, balki har Jumma ko.&#x27; Toh Ka&#x27;ab ne Taurat padhi aur kaha, &#x27;Sach farmaya Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne.&#x27; Abu Huraira (Radi Allahu Anhu) kehte hain, phir main Basra bin Abi Basra Ghifari se mila, unhone pucha kahan se aa rahe ho? Maine kaha Koh-e-Toor se. Unhone kaha, &#x27;Agar tum mujhe milte toh tum wahan na jate. Maine Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) se suna, aap farmate the: &#x27;Sirf teen masjido&#x27;n ki taraf hi sawari ki jaye: Masjidi Haram, meri yeh masjid aur Masjidi Iliya ya Baitul Muqaddas&#x27; (rawi ko shaq hai). Abu Huraira (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne kaha, phir main Abdullah bin Salam se mila aur unhe woh guftagu batayi jo Ka&#x27;ab se Jumma ke bare mein hui thi, aur yeh bhi ke Ka&#x27;ab ne kaha tha yeh saal mein ek baar hota hai. Abdullah bin Salam ne kaha, &#x27;Ka&#x27;ab ne jhoot bola.&#x27; Phir maine bataya ke Ka&#x27;ab ne Taurat padh kar kaha ke yeh sa&#x27;at har Jumma ko hoti hai, toh Abdullah bin Salam ne kaha, &#x27;Sach kaha Ka&#x27;ab ne.&#x27; Phir Abdullah bin Salam ne kaha, &#x27;Main janta hoon woh sa&#x27;at kaunsi hai.&#x27; Abu Huraira (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne kaha, &#x27;Mujhe batao aur bakhil na bano.&#x27; Abdullah bin Salam ne kaha, &#x27;Woh Jumma ki aakhri sa&#x27;at hai.&#x27; Abu Huraira (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne kaha, &#x27;Woh aakhri sa&#x27;at kaise ho sakti hai jabke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya hai ke musalman banda namaz mein ho tabhi mangta hai? Yeh sa&#x27;at toh aisi hai ke is mein namaz nahi ho sakti?&#x27; Abdullah bin Salam ne jawab diya, &#x27;Kya Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne nahi farmaya ke jo shakhs namaz ke intezar mein baitha hai woh namaz mein hi hai jab tak namaz na padh le?&#x27; Abu Huraira (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne kaha, &#x27;Haan.&#x27; Abdullah bin Salam ne kaha, &#x27;Bas yahi matlab hai.&#x27;</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 239]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/239" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 239

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A240)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 5</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ يَحْيَى بْنِ سَعِيدٍ، أَنَّهُ بَلَغَهُ أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ مَا عَلَى أَحَدِكُمْ لَوِ اتَّخَذَ ثَوْبَيْنِ لِجُمُعَتِهِ سِوَى ثَوْبَىْ مَهْنَتِهِ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 240]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/240" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Yahya ibn Said that he had heard that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;There is nothing wrong in wearing two garments which are not work-clothes for jumua</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 240]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/240" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="auto">याहया बिन सईद अल-अंसारी से रिवायत है के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया क्या नुक्सान है अगर वह अपने रोज़-मर्रा के कपड़ो&#x27;ं के अलावा जुम्मे की नमाज़ के लिए अलग कपडे बना ले?&#x27;</p>

## Narration 240

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A241)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 5</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: عَبْدَ اللَّهِ بْنَ عُمَرَ (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ نَافِعٍ، أَنَّ عَبْدَ اللَّهِ بْنَ عُمَرَ، كَانَ لاَ يَرُوحُ إِلَى الْجُمُعَةِ إِلاَّ ادَّهَنَ وَتَطَيَّبَ إِلاَّ أَنْ يَكُونَ حَرَامًا ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 241]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/241" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Abdullah ibn Umar (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Nafi that Abdullah ibn Umar would never go to jumua without wearing oil and perfume except when it was forbidden (i.e. when he was muhrim)</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 241]</p>

## Narration 241

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A242)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 5</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ (companion). Abu Hurayra’s attributed saying is retained; the following compiler rule, where supplied, remains context.</p>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ أَبِي بَكْرِ بْنِ حَزْمٍ، عَمَّنْ حَدَّثَهُ عَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ، أَنَّهُ كَانَ يَقُولُ لأَنْ يُصَلِّيَ أَحَدُكُمْ بِظَهْرِ الْحَرَّةِ خَيْرٌ لَهُ مِنْ أَنْ يَقْعُدَ حَتَّى إِذَا قَامَ الإِمَامُ يَخْطُبُ جَاءَ يَتَخَطَّى رِقَابَ النَّاسِ يَوْمَ الْجُمُعَةِ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 242]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/242" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 242</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   242 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl"> قَالَ مَالِكٌ السُّنَّةُ عِنْدَنَا أَنْ يَسْتَقْبِلَ النَّاسُ الإِمَامَ يَوْمَ الْجُمُعَةِ إِذَا أَرَادَ أَنْ يَخْطُبَ مَنْ كَانَ مِنْهُمْ يَلِي الْقِبْلَةَ وَغَيْرَهَا ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/242" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Abu Hurayra (companion). Abu Hurayra’s attributed saying is retained; the following compiler rule, where supplied, remains context.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Nafi from Abdullah ibn Abi Bakr ibn Hazm from whoever related it to him that Abu Hurayra used to say, &quot;It is better for a man to pray on the surface of al Harra (a rocky area in Madina) than for him to wait until the imam stands to give the khutba and then come and step over people&#x27;s necks.&quot;</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 242]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/242" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 242</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   242 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr"> Malik said, &quot;The sunna with us is that the people face the imam on the day of jumua when he intends to give the khutba, whether they are near the qibla or elsewhere</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/242" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">अबू हुरैराह (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) ने कहा के अगर कोई तुम में से नमाज़ पढ़े ज़ोहर, तो बेहतर है इस से के बैठा रहे अपने घर में, फिर जब इमाम ख़ुत्बा पढ़ने को खड़ा हो, आये फांदता हुआ गर्दनों को लोगों की दिन जुमुआ के।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/242" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: ابوہریرہ (companion). Abu Hurayra’s attributed saying is retained; the following compiler rule, where supplied, remains context.</p>

<p dir="rtl">ابوہریرہ نے کہا کہ اگر کوئی تم میں سے نماز پڑھے ظہر جرہ میں بہتر ہے اس سے کہ بیٹھا رہے اپنے گھر میں پھر جب امام خطبہ پڑھنے کو کھڑا ہو آئے پھاندتا ہوا گردنوں کو لوگوں کی دن جمعہ کے ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 242]</p>

## Narration 242

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A243)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 5</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ ضَمْرَةَ بْنِ سَعِيدٍ الْمَازِنِيِّ، عَنْ عُبَيْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عُتْبَةَ بْنِ مَسْعُودٍ، أَنَّ الضَّحَّاكَ بْنَ قَيْسٍ، سَأَلَ النُّعْمَانَ بْنَ بَشِيرٍ مَاذَا كَانَ يَقْرَأُ بِهِ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم يَوْمَ الْجُمُعَةِ عَلَى إِثْرِ سُورَةِ الْجُمُعَةِ قَالَ كَانَ يَقْرَأُ ‏{‏هَلْ أَتَاكَ حَدِيثُ الْغَاشِيَةِ‏}‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/243" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: prophetic report. Attribution: Muhammad, as explicitly attributed in this complete source field. Exact English field independently read in full. This locally supported Prophetic report is preserved as its own exact source passage; companion/jurist discussion outside the selected passage remains source context. No authenticity or translation-equivalence judgment is made.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Damra ibn Said al-Mazini from Ubaydullah ibn Abdullah ibn Utba ibn Masud that ad-Dahhak ibn Qays asked an-Numan ibn Bashir, &quot;What did the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, use to recite on the day of jumua after suratal-Jumua (Sura 62)?&quot; He said, &quot;He used to recite al-Ghashiya (Sura)</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 243]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/243" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 243

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A244)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 5</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ صَفْوَانَ بْنِ سُلَيْمٍ، - قَالَ مَالِكٌ لاَ أَدْرِي أَعَنِ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم أَمْ لاَ - أَنَّهُ قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ مَنْ تَرَكَ الْجُمُعَةَ ثَلاَثَ مَرَّاتٍ مِنْ غَيْرِ عُذْرٍ وَلاَ عِلَّةٍ طَبَعَ اللَّهُ عَلَى قَلْبِهِ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 244]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/244" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 244</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   244 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that Safwan ibn Sulaym said, &quot;If anyone omits jumua three times without reason or illness, Allah will set a seal on his heart.&quot; (Malik said, &quot;I don&#x27;t know if it was from the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, or not)</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 244]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/244" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 244</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   244 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">सफ़वान बिन सालीम से रिवायत है लेकिन मालिक कहते हैं कि मुझे मालूम नहीं रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) से नक़ल किया या नहीं. कहा जो शख्स चोर डेगा जमुआह को तीन मर्तबा बग़ैर उज़्र और बीमारी के ता&#x27;अला उसके दिल पर.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 244]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/244" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">صفوان بن سلیم سے روایت ہے لیکن مالک کہتے ہیں کہ مجھے معلوم نہیں رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم سے نقل کیا یا نہیں۔ کہا جو شخص چھوڑ دے گا جمعہ کو تین مرتبہ بغیر عذر اور بیماری کے مہر کر دے گا اللہ تعالیٰ اس کے دل پر ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 244]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/244" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Safwan bin Saleem se riwayat hai lekin Malik kehte hain ke mujhe maloom nahi Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) se naqal kiya ya nahi. Kaha jo shakhs chor dega Jumuah ko teen martaba baghair uzr aur bimari ke, mohar kar dega Allah Ta&#x27;ala uske dil par.</p>

## Narration 244

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A245)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 5</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ جَعْفَرِ بْنِ مُحَمَّدٍ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم خَطَبَ خُطْبَتَيْنِ يَوْمَ الْجُمُعَةِ وَجَلَسَ بَيْنَهُمَا ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 245]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/245" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Jafar ibn Muhammad from his father that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, gave two khutbas on the day of jumua and sat down between them</p>

## Narration 245

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A246)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 6</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، عَنْ عُرْوَةَ بْنِ الزُّبَيْرِ، عَنْ عَائِشَةَ، زَوْجِ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم صَلَّى فِي الْمَسْجِدِ ذَاتَ لَيْلَةٍ فَصَلَّى بِصَلاَتِهِ نَاسٌ ثُمَّ صَلَّى اللَّيْلَةَ الْقَابِلَةَ فَكَثُرَ النَّاسُ ثُمَّ اجْتَمَعُوا مِنَ اللَّيْلَةِ الثَّالِثَةِ أَوِ الرَّابِعَةِ فَلَمْ يَخْرُجْ إِلَيْهِمْ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم فَلَمَّا أَصْبَحَ قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ قَدْ رَأَيْتُ الَّذِي صَنَعْتُمْ وَلَمْ يَمْنَعْنِي مِنَ الْخُرُوجِ إِلَيْكُمْ إِلاَّ أَنِّي خَشِيتُ أَنْ تُفْرَضَ عَلَيْكُمْ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ وَذَلِكَ فِي رَمَضَانَ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 246]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/246" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 246</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   246 ]</p>

</details>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Ibn Shihab from Urwa ibn az- Zubayr from A&#x27;isha, the wife of the Prophet, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, prayed in the mosque one night and people prayed behind him. Then he prayed the next night and there were more people. Then they gathered on the third or fourth night and the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, did not come out to them. In the morning, he said, &quot;I saw what you were doing and the only thing that prevented me from coming out to you was that I feared that it would become obligatory (fard) for you.&quot; This happened in Ramadan</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 246]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/246" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 246</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   246 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">उम्मुल मोमिनीन हज़रत आयशा (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हा) से रिवायत है के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने नमाज़ पढ़ी मस्जिद में एक रात, तो नमाज़ पढ़ी पीछे आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) के लोगों ने। फिर दूसरी रात में इसी तरह पढ़ी तो लोग बहुत आये। फिर लोग तीसरी या चौथी रात जमा हुए लेकिन नबी (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) (तरावीह) के लिये नहीं निकले। जब सुबह हुई तो आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया, मैं ने देखा जो तुमने किया लेकिन मुझे किसी चीज़ ने निकलने से नहीं रोका सिवाये इस ख़ौफ़ से के कहीं ये (तरावीह) तुम पर फ़र्ज़ न हो जाये, और ये क़िस्सा रमज़ान में था।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 246]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/246" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">ام المومنین حضرت عائشہ رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہا روایت ہے کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے نماز پڑھی مسجد میں ایک رات تو نماز پڑھی پیچھے آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کے لوگوں نے پھر دوسری رات میں اسی طرح پڑھی تو لوگ بہت آئے پھر لوگ تیسری یا چوتھی رات جمع ہوئے لیکن نبی صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم ( تراویح ) کے لئے نہیں نکلے ۔ جب صبح ہوئی تو آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا میں نے دیکھا جو تم نے کیا لیکن مجھے کسی چیز نے نکلنے سے نہیں روکا سوائے اس خوف سے کہ کہیں یہ ( تراویح ) تم پر فرض نہ ہوجائے اور یہ قصہ رمضان میں تھا۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 246]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/246" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Umm-ul-Momineen Hazrat Ayesha (Radi Allahu Anha) se riwayat hai ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne namaz parhi masjid mein ek raat, to namaz parhi peeche aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ke logon ne. Phir dusri raat mein isi tarah parhi to log bohot aaye. Phir log teesri ya chauthi raat jama hue lekin Nabi (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) (taraveeh) ke liye nahi nikle. Jab subah hui to aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya, main ne dekha jo tumne kiya lekin mujhe kisi cheez ne nikalne se nahi roka siwaye is khauf se ke kahin ye (taraveeh) tum par farz na ho jaye, aur ye qissa Ramazan mein tha.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 246]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/246" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 246

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A247)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 6</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، عَنْ أَبِي سَلَمَةَ بْنِ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنِ عَوْفٍ، عَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم كَانَ يُرَغِّبُ فِي قِيَامِ رَمَضَانَ مِنْ غَيْرِ أَنْ يَأْمُرَ بِعَزِيمَةٍ فَيَقُولُ ‏ &quot;‏ مَنْ قَامَ رَمَضَانَ إِيمَانًا وَاحْتِسَابًا غُفِرَ لَهُ مَا تَقَدَّمَ مِنْ ذَنْبِهِ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ قَالَ ابْنُ شِهَابٍ فَتُوُفِّيَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم وَالأَمْرُ عَلَى ذَلِكَ ثُمَّ كَانَ الأَمْرُ عَلَى ذَلِكَ فِي خِلاَفَةِ أَبِي بَكْرٍ وَصَدْرًا مِنْ خِلاَفَةِ عُمَرَ بْنِ الْخَطَّابِ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 247]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/247" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>
