<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Ibn Shihab from Abu Salama ibn Abd ar-Rahman ibn Awf from Abu Hurayra that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, used to exhort people to watch the night in prayer in Ramadan but never ordered it definitely. He used to say, &quot;Whoever watches the night in prayer in Ramadan with trust and expectancy, will be forgiven all his previous wrong actions.&quot; Ibn Shihab said, &quot;The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, died while that was still the custom, and it continued to be the custom in the caliphate of Abu Bakr and at the beginning of the caliphate of Umar ibn al-Khattab</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 247]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/247" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अबू हुरैराह (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) रग़बत दिलाते थे लोगों को तरावीह पढ़ने की रातों को और न हुक्म करते थे बतौरे वाजिब के, तो फ़रमाते थे आप: जिसने तरावीह पढ़ी रमज़ान में उसको हक़ समझ कर, ख़ास अल्लाह के लिये, बख़्शे जायेंगे अगले गुनाह उसके। कहा इब्ने शहाब ने: पस वफ़ात हुई रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) की और ऐसा ही हाल रहा, फिर ऐसा ही हाल रहा हज़रत अबू बक्र (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) की ख़िलाफ़त में और शुरू शुरू हज़रत उमर (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) की ख़िलाफ़त में।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 247]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/247" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">ابوہریرہ رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ روایت ہے کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم رغبت دلاتے تھے لوگوں کو تراویح پڑھنے کی راتوں کو اور نہ حکم کرتے تھے بطور واجب کے تو فرماتے تھے آپ جس نے ترایح پڑھی رمضان میں اس کو حق سمجھ کر خاص اللہ کے لئے بخشے جائیں گے اگلے گناہ اس کے کہا ابن شہاب نے پس وفات ہوئی رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کی اور ایسا ہی حال رہا پھر ایسا ہی حال رہا حضرت ابوبکر کی خلافت میں اور شروع شروع حضرت عمر کی خلافت میں</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 247]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/247" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Abu Hurairah (Radi Allahu Anhu) se riwayat hai ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) raghbat dilate the logon ko taraveeh parhne ki raaton ko aur na hukum karte the bator-e-wajib ke, to farmate the aap: jisne taraveeh parhi Ramazan mein usko haq samajh kar, khaas Allah ke liye, bakhshe jayenge agle gunah uske. Kaha Ibn-e-Shahab ne: Pas wafat hui Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ki aur aisa hi haal raha, phir aisa hi haal raha Hazrat Abu Bakr (Radi Allahu Anhu) ki khilafat mein aur shuru shuru Hazrat Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) ki khilafat mein.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 247]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/247" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 247

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A248)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 6</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: عُمَرَ بْنِ الْخَطَّابِ (companion). The report of Umar’s action and speech is retained. The following explanatory time gloss remains source context, including its own supplied wording rather than a correction inferred from other languages.</p>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي مَالِكٌ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، عَنْ عُرْوَةَ بْنِ الزُّبَيْرِ، عَنْ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنِ عَبْدٍ الْقَارِيِّ، أَنَّهُ قَالَ خَرَجْتُ مَعَ عُمَرَ بْنِ الْخَطَّابِ فِي رَمَضَانَ إِلَى الْمَسْجِدِ فَإِذَا النَّاسُ أَوْزَاعٌ مُتَفَرِّقُونَ يُصَلِّي الرَّجُلُ لِنَفْسِهِ وَيُصَلِّي الرَّجُلُ فَيُصَلِّي بِصَلاَتِهِ الرَّهْطُ فَقَالَ عُمَرُ وَاللَّهِ إِنِّي لأَرَانِي لَوْ جَمَعْتُ هَؤُلاَءِ عَلَى قَارِئٍ وَاحِدٍ لَكَانَ أَمْثَلَ ‏.‏ فَجَمَعَهُمْ عَلَى أُبَىِّ بْنِ كَعْبٍ - قَالَ - ثُمَّ خَرَجْتُ مَعَهُ لَيْلَةً أُخْرَى وَالنَّاسُ يُصَلُّونَ بِصَلاَةِ قَارِئِهِمْ فَقَالَ عُمَرُ نِعْمَتِ الْبِدْعَةُ هَذِهِ وَالَّتِي تَنَامُونَ عَنْهَا أَفْضَلُ مِنَ الَّتِي تَقُومُونَ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 248]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/248" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   248 ]</p>

</details>

<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl"> يَعْنِي آخِرَ اللَّيْلِ وَكَانَ النَّاسُ يَقُومُونَ أَوَّلَهُ ‏.‏</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Umar ibn alKhattab (companion). The report of Umar’s action and speech is retained. The following explanatory time gloss remains source context, including its own supplied wording rather than a correction inferred from other languages.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Malik related to me from Ibn Shihab from Urwa ibn az-Zubayr that Abd ar-Rahman ibn Abd al-Qari said, &quot;I went out with Umar ibn alKhattab in Ramadan to the mosque and the people there were spread out in groups. Some men were praying by themselves, whilst others were praying in small groups. Umar said, &#x27;By Allah! It would be better in my opinion if these people gathered behind one reciter.&#x27; So he gathered them behind Ubayy ibn Kab. Then I went out with him another night and the people were praying behind their Qur&#x27;an reciter. Umar said, &#x27;How excellent this new way is, but what you miss while you are asleep is better than what you watch in prayer.&#x27;</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 248]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/248" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 248</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   248 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr"> He meant the end of the night, and people used to watch the beginning of the night in prayer</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 248]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/248" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Hindi · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 248</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   248 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">अब्दुर रहमान बिन अब्दुल् कारी से रिवायत है के मैं निकला उमर बिन ख़त्ताब (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) के साथ रमज़ान में मस्जिद को, तो देखा के लोग जुदा जुदा मुतफ़र्रिक़ पढ़ रहे हैं, किसी शख़्स के साथ आठ दस आदमी पढ़ रहे हैं। तो कहा उमर (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) ने, क़सम अल्लाह की मुझे मालूम होता है के अगर इन सब को एक क़ारी के पीछे कर दूँ तो अच्छा हो। फिर इन सब को उबय बिन काअब के पीछे कर दिया। कहा अब्दुर रहमान ने, फिर जब दूसरी रात को मैं उनके साथ आया तो देखा के सब लोग उबय बिन काअब के पीछे नमाज़ पढ़ रहे हैं, तब कहा हज़रत उमर (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) ने: अच्छी है ये बिदअत, और जिस वक़्त तुम सोते हो वो बेहतर है उस वक़्त से जब नमाज़ पढ़ते हो, यानी अव्वल रात, और लोग खड़े होते थे अव्वल रात में।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/248" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: عمر بن خطاب (companion). The report of Umar’s action and speech is retained. The following explanatory time gloss remains source context, including its own supplied wording rather than a correction inferred from other languages.</p>

<p dir="rtl">عبدالر حمن بن عبدالقاری سے روایت ہے کہ میں نکلا عمر بن خطاب کے ساتھ رمضان میں مسجد کو تو دیکھا کہ لوگ جدا جدا متفرق پڑھ رہے ہیں کسی شخص کے ساتھ آٹھ دس آدمی پڑھ رہے ہیں تو کہا عمر نے قسم اللہ کی مجھے معلوم ہوتا ہے کہ اگر ان سب کو ایک قاری کے پیچھے کردوں تو اچھا ہو پھر ان سب کو ابی بن کعب کے پیچھے کر دیا کہا عبدالرحمن نے پھر جب دوسری رات کو میں ان کے ساتھ آیا تو دیکھا کہ سب لوگ ابی بن کعب کے پیچھے نماز پڑھ رہے ہیں تب کہا حضرت عمر نے اچھی ہے یہ بدعت اور جس وقت تم سوتے ہو وہ بہتر ہے اس وقت سے جب نماز پڑھتے ہو</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 248]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/248" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Abdur Rahman bin Abd-ul-Qari se riwayat hai ke main nikla Umar bin Khattab (Radi Allahu Anhu) ke saath Ramazan mein masjid ko, to dekha ke log juda juda mutafarriq parh rahe hain, kisi shakhs ke saath aath das aadmi parh rahe hain. To kaha Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne, qasam Allah ki mujhe maloom hota hai ke agar in sab ko ek qari ke peeche kar doon to achha ho. Phir in sab ko Ubay bin Ka&#x27;ab ke peeche kar diya. Kaha Abdur Rahman ne, phir jab dusri raat ko main unke saath aaya to dekha ke sab log Ubay bin Ka&#x27;ab ke peeche namaz parh rahe hain, tab kaha Hazrat Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne: achhi hai ye bid&#x27;at, aur jis waqt tum sote ho wo behtar hai us waqt se jab namaz parhte ho, yani awwal raat, aur log khare hote the awwal raat mein.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/248" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

## Narration 248

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A249)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 6</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: عُمَرُ بْنُ الْخَطَّابِ (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ مُحَمَّدِ بْنِ يُوسُفَ، عَنِ السَّائِبِ بْنِ يَزِيدَ، أَنَّهُ قَالَ أَمَرَ عُمَرُ بْنُ الْخَطَّابِ أُبَىَّ بْنَ كَعْبٍ وَتَمِيمًا الدَّارِيَّ أَنْ يَقُومَا، لِلنَّاسِ بِإِحْدَى عَشْرَةَ رَكْعَةً قَالَ وَقَدْ كَانَ الْقَارِئُ يَقْرَأُ بِالْمِئِينَ حَتَّى كُنَّا نَعْتَمِدُ عَلَى الْعِصِيِّ مِنْ طُولِ الْقِيَامِ وَمَا كُنَّا نَنْصَرِفُ إِلاَّ فِي فُرُوعِ الْفَجْرِ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 249]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/249" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Umar ibn al-Khattab (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Muhammad ibn Yusuf that as- Sa&#x27;ib ibn Yazid said, &quot;Umar ibn al-Khattab ordered Ubayy ibn Kab and Tamim ad-Dari to watch the night in prayer with the people for eleven rakas.The reciterof the Qur&#x27;an would recite the Mi&#x27;in (a group of medium-sized suras) until we would be leaning on our staffs from having stood so long in prayer. And we would not leave until the approach of dawn</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 249]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/249" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">साइब बिन यज़ीद से रिवायत है के हुकुम किया हज़रत उमर (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) ने उबई बिन कअब और तमीम दारी को ग्यारह रकात पढ़ाने का। कहा साइब बिन यज़ीद ने, कहा इमाम पढ़ता था सौ सौ आयतें एक रकात में यहां तक के हम सहारा लगाते थे लकड़ी पर और नहीं फारिग होते थे हम मगर करीब-ए-फ़ज्र के।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/249" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: حضرت عمر (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="rtl">سائب بن یزید سے روایت ہے کہ حکم کیا حضرت عمر نے ابی بن کعب اور تمیم داری کو گیارہ رکعت پڑھانے کا کہا سائب بن یزید نے کہا امام پڑھتا تھا سو سو آیتیں ایک رکعت میں یہاں تک کہ ہم سہارا لگاتے تھے لکڑی پر اور نہیں فارغ ہوتے تھے ہم مگر قریب فجر کے ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 249]</p>

## Narration 249

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A250)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 6</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: historical report. Attribution: يَزِيدَ بْنِ رُومَانَ (successor). The named witness’s report of practice in Umar’s time is retained exactly. A trailing explanatory breakdown, where present, remains source context.</p>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ يَزِيدَ بْنِ رُومَانَ، أَنَّهُ قَالَ كَانَ النَّاسُ يَقُومُونَ فِي زَمَانِ عُمَرَ بْنِ الْخَطَّابِ فِي رَمَضَانَ بِثَلاَثٍ وَعِشْرِينَ رَكْعَةً ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 250]</p>

## Narration 250

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A251)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 6</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: successor report. Attribution: الأَعْرَجَ (successor). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ دَاوُدَ بْنِ الْحُصَيْنِ، أَنَّهُ سَمِعَ الأَعْرَجَ، يَقُولُ مَا أَدْرَكْتُ النَّاسَ إِلاَّ وَهُمْ يَلْعَنُونَ الْكَفَرَةَ فِي رَمَضَانَ ‏.‏ قَالَ وَكَانَ الْقَارِئُ يَقْرَأُ سُورَةَ الْبَقَرَةِ فِي ثَمَانِ رَكَعَاتٍ فَإِذَا قَامَ بِهَا فِي اثْنَتَىْ عَشْرَةَ رَكْعَةً رَأَى النَّاسُ أَنَّهُ قَدْ خَفَّفَ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 251]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/251" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 251</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   251 ]</p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: successor report. Attribution: al-Araj (successor). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Da&#x27;ud ibn al-Husayn that he heard al-Araj say, &quot;I never saw the people in Ramadan, but that they were cursing the disbelievers.&quot; He added, &quot;The reciter of Qur&#x27;an used to recite surat al-Baqara in eight rakas and if he did it in twelve rakas the people would think that he had made it easy</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 251]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/251" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">दाऊद बिन हुसैन ने सुना अब्दुर रहमान बिन हुरमुज़ आ&#x27;रज से कहते थे मैं ने पाया लोगों को लानत करते हुए काफ़िरों पर रमज़ान में, और इमाम पढ़ता था सूरह बक़रह आठ रकातों में, जब बारह रकातों में पढ़ता था तो लोगों को मालूम होता था के तख़फ़ीफ़ की।</p>

<p dir="auto">Report type: successor report. Attribution: عبدالرحمن بن ہرمز اعرج (successor). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="rtl">داؤد بن حصین نے سنا عبدالرحمن بن ہرمز اعرج سے کہتے تھے میں نے پایا لوگوں کو لعنت کرتے تھے کافروں پر رمضان میں اور امام پڑھتا تھا سورت بقرہ آٹھ رکعتوں میں جب بارہ رکعتوں میں پڑھتا تھا تو لوگوں کو معلوم ہوتا تھا کہ تخفیف کی۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 251]</p>
